NEW `viewmodel/WatchHistoryViewModel.kt` — Hilt, same source as the rail (`watchHistoryDao.observeRecent` + `syncedProgressDao.observeRecent` + refused ids, `AdultPolicy.listableRows`). NEW `WatchHistoryMerge.mergeAll` — the rail's newest-wins fold without its resumable filter, so the two surfaces can never disagree about where a title stopped, only about whether it is worth resuming. NEW route `Screen.WatchHistory("watch_history")`; entry points in My List (TopAppBar history action, visible in every list state) and Settings (Watch history card before Help). Verified genuinely missing first: no history screen or route existed; the three "history" hits were comments/copy only.
- **Test.** NEW `WatchHistoryRowTest` — 11 tests pinning the row mapping (scope label, progress fraction/label, finished vs barely-started vs unknown-duration, date shape, untappable at `postId 0`) and the shared fold (`mergeAll` keeps a finished row the rail drops, folds a newer synced position, drops an unknown synced scope, newest-first + limit).

**Fix — pre-push review of 40370e2 (main chat, 04:31 PKT).** The `WatchHistoryRowTest` case "a title watched to the end says finished, not a time left" asserted `progressLabel` is null for a row at 115 s of 120 s — but the rail's `progressLabel` answers "Less than a minute left" there (5 s remain, under the 60 s bar), so the test would fail in CI. Fixed in the mapper, not the test: `WatchHistoryRow.of` now reports no time left once a row is finished, so a finished row can never show a time-left label next to "Finished". Also removed the `WatchHistoryDao.observeAll()` query added during the 04:24 collision — the ViewModel reads `observeRecent(HISTORY_LIMIT * 2)`, so it had no caller. Pre-push checks re-run on the fix below.

**Fix — CI red on 784ed8b (self-check, 04:38 PKT).** Android run 42312530361 failed at `:app:compileDebugUnitTestKotlin`: `WatchHistoryRowTest.kt:96,138 No parameter with name 'workRef' found`. The test's `row()` helper hardcoded `workRef = "work-7"` while two `mergeAll` call sites passed `workRef = "work-8"` — the helper never forwarded it. Fixed in the test only: `row()` takes `workRef: String = "work-7"` and forwards it to `WatchHistoryEntity` (workRef is the entity's primary-key component, so the two merge tests need distinct refs to mean anything). No production code touched, no imports changed, braces/parens balanced. This was the 04:32 run's own Session 81 test, not the sibling's file — the sibling's 784ed8b touched only the mapper, the DAO and this log.

## Session 85 — Day 2 plan, D2.1: New-phone progress pull — the proof (2026-11-07 ~06:10 PKT)

**What:** the pull already existed — `SettingsViewModel.quickSignIn()` calls `syncNow()` on success (verified in code 06:00 PKT), and `syncNow()` folds `listProgress` into the `SyncedProgress` mirror, server-wins by timestamp. What was missing was the proof: no test referenced `quickSignIn` or `syncNow`. Debate with agy3 closed 06:04 PKT (it agrees, no gap in the pull; `PlayerViewModel` checks `resumePositionMs` before playback, and `WatchHistoryMerge` intentionally filters server-pulled rows with no local `WatchHistoryEntity`, so no ghost cards). This session builds the proof, not the feature.

- **Test.** NEW `sync/NewPhoneProgressPullTest.kt` — 3 Robolectric tests against a hand-rolled `ServerSocket` fake that speaks the same wire shape as `accounts_api.py` (mint/import on POST `/api/v2/account`, empty favorites, entitlement, newest-wins progress store). The real Retrofit client, the real Gson wire models and the real Room in-memory DAOs all run — no mocked API layer. (1) Device A mints an account and reports 123,456 ms of 600,000 ms; device B, a fresh in-memory Room with zero local history, signs in with the same key, `syncNow()` pulls exactly 1 row, and `resumePositionMs` returns 123,456 exactly. (2) A second `syncNow()` pulls 0 rows — the same stamp is not a new pull. (3) A stale server row never overwrites a newer local row: device B's mirror seeded directly with a newer stamp keeps 200,000 ms after the pull. Two review catches fixed before committing: Gson writes large doubles in scientific notation (`client_updated_at` ~1.79E9), so the fake's number parser accepts exponents; and the device helper returns a `Pair<repo, db>` instead of reaching for `databases.last()`.
- **Not built:** no production code changed — the feature was already there, and rebuilding it would have been the check-before-creating failure this plan exists to prevent.

**Fix — CI red on 42323050286 (self-check, ~06:30 PKT).** Android run failed at `:app:compileDebugUnitTestKotlin`: `Unresolved reference 'HttpServer'` — `com.sun.net.httpserver` lives in the `jdk.httpserver` module, which the unit-test compile classpath does not resolve. Fixed in the test only: the fake is now a hand-rolled HTTP/1.1 server on a plain `ServerSocket` (`java.base` only — one request per connection, `Connection: close`, bodies read by `Content-Length`, daemon accept + connection threads), with the same routes and the same newest-wins store. No new dependency added; MockWebServer for one test would have been the heavier change. Re-push follows this fix.

**Fix — CI red on 42324051583 (self-check, ~06:45 PKT).** Compiles now, but all 3 new tests fail on device B's half (test 1: `quickSignIn` refuses; test 2: first pull is 0 rows; test 3: `syncNow` not completed) while device A's create + report + resume all pass — and the fake server's store provably holds device A's row. Cause not yet proven (the run log carries only the assertion lines; the suppressed Robolectric note about unexecuted main-looper runnables is unconfirmed as the mechanism, and no existing test has ever driven a real DataStore-backed `ServerSettingsRepository` under Robolectric). Fix in the test only: the fake server now keeps an ordered event log (every request + accept/handle error, with the accept loop surviving non-fatal errors instead of dying on them), and the failing assertions print the refusal outcome plus that log, so the next CI run names the culprit instead of guessing. Re-push follows this fix.

**Fix — CI red on 42325515306 (self-check, ~07:00 PKT).** The instrumented run named both culprits, both in the test, neither in production: (1) `quickSignIn` answered `Refused(WEAK_KEY)` — the minted key `"d2testkey12345678"` is 17 characters (`"d2testkey"` is 9, not 8) and the short form is exactly 16, so `quickLoginKey` refused it; fixed to `"d2testkey1234567"`. (2) `syncNow` died at `GET /api/v2/account/favorites?limit=1000 -> 404` — Retrofit appends the query string and the fake dispatched on the raw request target; fixed to dispatch on the path before `?`. The server log also cleared the fake server itself: it answered every request it received. Re-push follows this fix.

**Fix — CI red on 42327351067 (self-check, ~07:20 PKT).** All 3 tests still failed, and the `ci-reports-253` artifact named the culprit this time: the fake stored `StoredProgress(season=0, episode=0, positionSeconds=0.0, clientUpdatedAt=0.0)` — every number parsed as 0.0 while `work_ref` parsed fine. Cause, in the test only: `numberField`'s raw-string regex ended `...)?)"` — a stray closing quote copied from `stringField`, where the trailing quote is correct because the value IS quoted. JSON numbers are unquoted, so `"season_number":1,` never matched and every field fell back to 0.0; device B then pulled one row under scope (0, 0) and its mirror had nothing at (1, 2). Fixed by removing that one quote (pattern now ends `...)?)`); a Python simulation of both patterns against a realistic Gson body (`1.794017274606E9` stamp included) reproduces the all-zero store before the fix and parses all five fields after it. No production code touched, no imports changed. Re-push follows this fix; D2.1 is green when this run's Android CI passes.

**Checks:** new file only — no production Kotlin file edited, so no import diff is owed on edited files; every import in the new file is used. `/*` vs `*/` and braces/parens balanced. No new network data class → no deserialization test owed. No local Gradle run (owner order: GitHub Actions only) — CI judges the app. **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges — nothing is in flight). **D2.7 closed first:** Android run 42343631090 on `a57d035` completed SUCCESS; APK app-debug-256 (23,374,190 B). D2.7 is DONE. Qoder fleet still blocked (canary 10:33 PKT, `qfmodel unavailable`); agy5 idle at prompt after REPORT DONE, agy2 idle (quota).

**What:** the maintenance overlay's only exit was "Use a different server" — a wrongly-flagged server trapped the viewer until the watcher's five-minute tick re-read the flag. Check-before-creating found no Retry anywhere (grep of `MaintenanceScreen.kt` + `RemoteConfigWatcher.kt`).

- **Watcher.** `RemoteConfigWatcher.refreshNow()` — one immediate re-read of `/remote-config`, outside the loop. Tap-spam coalesces via an in-flight `AtomicBoolean` (one re-read at a time; a tap during a refresh is a no-op). The loop's own tick is not guarded by the flag, so a manual refresh racing the tick can double-fetch once — harmless, both writes derive from the same endpoint. Failure policy unchanged: a failed or malformed poll leaves the last good answer in place, so a server being restarted cannot flip the app out of maintenance on a network error.
- **Screen.** `MaintenanceScreen` gains `onRetry: (() -> Unit)? = null` and renders a filled `Button("Retry")` above the change-server `TextButton` when it is set. The locked notice (no callbacks) is unchanged, including its 24dp spacing; the gap between the two buttons is 16dp when both are present.
- **Wiring.** `MainActivity` injects the `RemoteConfigWatcher` (Hilt `@Singleton`, same graph as `RaddStreamApplication`) and passes `onRetry = { remoteConfigWatcher.refreshNow() }`. When the operator has lifted the switch the re-read writes `maintenanceMode = false` and the same `maintenanceMode` flow that put the notice up clears it — no navigation or state reset on this side.
- **Tests.** `ScreenSnapshotTest.maintenanceRetryRechecksTheFlag` — the Retry button is present, tapping it calls the re-check, snapshot `03_maintenance_retry` joins the two existing maintenance captures.

## Session 96 — Tracking A: app usage block + consent gate (2026-11-07 ~20:40 PKT)

**What:** the server's aggregates-only engine (`usage_stats.py`) and the v1 panel Usage page already existed — the app sent nothing. This session closes that gap on branch `tracking-a-20261107`, and nothing in it is a per-install timeline: the server still cannot tell what any one person watched. A first-run "Anonymous usage totals" dialog (`AnalyticsConsentDialog.kt`, new) asks once with plain-English draft text (totals only — plays, playback failures, crashes; never name, phone number, location, what you watch, what you search; no per-person record; changeable in Settings) — the exact wording is the owner's to revise, and both buttons are answers (it cannot be dismissed unanswered). `ServerSettingsRepository` gains `analyticsConsent` (accepted/declined/null = never asked) + `saveAnalyticsConsent`, and `installUuid()`/`ensureInstallUuid()` mint a random UUID only for a viewer who said yes (never a device id, IMEI, or SIM). The `ApiProvider` interceptor adds `X-Install-Id`, `X-App-Version`, `X-Device-Class`, `X-Region`, `X-Audio-Lang`, `X-Subtitle-Lang`, `Accept-Language` only when consented — declined/unasked viewers send byte-identical requests to before. `ProgressRequest` gains an optional `usage` block (Gson omits nulls); `SyncAccountRepository.reportProgress` snapshots the counters before the call, attaches them when non-empty, and acknowledges only on a written ack, so a failed write keeps the counters for the next attempt; coins earned on progress, daily login, and share are counted on the same ack path. New `Telemetry.kt` + `UsageCounters.kt`: a gate-checked counter store backed by SharedPreferences (synchronous `commit()`, because the crash handler and player callbacks cannot suspend), per-field cap 1,000,000, acknowledge subtracts exactly what was sent; `deviceClassFor` is RAM-only (under 3 GiB low, under 6 GiB mid, else high; no model string — a model string is a fingerprint); `installCrashHandler()` wraps the previous default handler and records crashes. The player counts every initial load and failover hop as a playback attempt (the auto-quality swap does not), and `onPlayerError` counts network failures/timeouts as buffer failures, anything else as a playback error. `VipViewModel.grantRewardedVip` counts a rewarded completion only — no coins are granted on it (Monetag native-Android server verification is unconfirmed; postback is documented for Telegram only). Settings gains an "Anonymous usage totals" card with a Switch right after the Disclaimer card; flipping it refreshes the snapshot at once, so off stops the next request's headers without a restart. `RaddStreamApplication` wires `Telemetry.init` + the crash handler + a background snapshot refresh in `onCreate`; `MainActivity` renders the dialog when the answer is still null. Server side: `usage_stats.py` gains `region`, `audio_lang`, `subtitle_lang` dims with an auto-migration on `usage_device` (old rows read 'unknown'; the live DB gains the columns on the first account call after deploy, no separate step); geo still comes only from Accept-Language, never IP.

## Session 98 — Emulator findings: Play honesty, server-link leak, daily check-in on Home, browser strip (2026-11-07 ~23:00 PKT)

**What:** user order 22:51 PKT ("Fix all of these now") after the Appetize emulator test of APK 267 found four things. **Play:** the two series titles the tester opened genuinely have no playable copy on the server — live API proof: Alba post 150795 and Alchemy of Souls post 150797 have ALL tiers `download_only`, 0 playable — so the grey Play button was the server telling the truth, but the app never said so: the episode row had no click handler at all (tapping it did nothing), and the play triangle showed whenever a best print existed even when it could not start. No change to the server resolver, `VipQualityGate`, or the 2s V2 timeout — `quality_gate` is OFF live (remote-config false), so no VIP blocking is involved. **Server link:** `SetupScreen` printed the raw server URL prefilled on first run, and the silent probe tried once with a 15s bound (2 of 4 emulator sessions fell through to Setup on a slow network). (or "No catalog server set.") + a [Change] button instead of the raw URL in plain sight (`SettingsViewModel.showServerField`); change-server still works the same once revealed. **Daily check-in on Home:** it lived only inside the VIP screen, never device-verified. `HomeViewModel` now observes the same facts (`loginStreakCount`, `signedIn`, `loadStreakLadder()`/`loadCoinBalance()`) and `claimDailyLogin()` mirrors the VIP screen's call, guards and words exactly; `HomeScreen` gains a compact `DailyCheckInCard` under the search bar ("Daily check-in — Day N of 7", today's coins, [Claim +N coins] / "Claimed" / "Sign in to claim", "Wallet: N coins"), rendered only once the server ladder arrives. The VIP screen is untouched. **Browser identity on Home:** a "Media browser" strip under the check-in card with three existing routes only — "Play a video link" (Search, where pasted direct video links already play via `DirectVideoUrl`), "On this phone" (`Screen.LocalFiles`), "Downloads" (`Screen.Downloads` / Media Vault) — wired in `NavGraph`. ## Session 99 — Retest follow-ups: claim button, Search paste hint, player give-up (2026-11-07 ~23:35 PKT)

**What:** three follow-ups from the APK 270 emulator retest (23:30 PKT). **Claim button:** the Home check-in button promised "Claim +N coins" from the local streak count, but the local `loginStreakCount` counts days the app was OPENED while the server counts days CLAIMED — on a fresh install it promised +20 (ladder day 2) and the server paid +10 (day 1). Fixed in `HomeScreen.kt`: the button now says "Claim today's coins" (the VIP screen's wording, no amount) and the now-unused `today`/`todaysCoins` locals are gone; the "Day N of 7" line and the server-confirmed result message ("+N coins — Day N of 7") are unchanged. A grep confirms no other "Claim +" promise exists in the app. **Search paste hint:** the "Play a video link" Home tile opens Search, but Search only reacted AFTER a URL was typed (`classifyPastedText`), with no hint that a link could be pasted — the retester saw no link-entry field. `SearchScreen.kt` now shows one honest line while the query is empty: "You can also paste a direct video link (.mp4 or .m3u8) here to play it in the app." The YouTube line ("YouTube links can't play in Radd...") is untouched, and nothing promises general web browsing. **Player give-up:** Pushpa 2's player opened black with the buffering spinner until the 3-minute session expired — no error, no give-up. The give-up card itself already existed (`GiveUpCard`: "Taking too long to start" / "This copy did not start within 30 seconds." + Retry / Try another print / Open with another player / Close) and the service-side error path already ends in it (`onPlayerError` → failover → `markGaveUp`). The hole was the start-deadline clock: the ticker disarmed the attempt on `c.isPlaying || c.duration > 0`, and ExoPlayer learns the duration from the playlist or file header while the picture is still black — so a stalled copy disarmed the 30s deadline at once, the start notice stood down on the same duration signal, and only the spinner was left. Fixed: new pure `StartDeadline.hasFirstFrame(playbackState)` — READY only — and the ticker uses it; a paused player still reaches READY, so pausing before the first frame does not trip the deadline. A copy that never renders now hits the 30s deadline and the give-up card owns the screen. No change to the 2s V2 timeout, the resolver, or any server code — the slow/dead resolve itself (25s then 404 on the Pushpa 2 1080p stream id) stays a separate server session (warm cache / failover).

**Checks:** import diff vs `b50887bd` is exactly 3 additions (`androidx.media3.common.Player` in `StartDeadline.kt` + the test, `assertFalse` in the test), 0 dropped, every new import used; @Composable counts unchanged (HomeScreen 10 → 10, SearchScreen 7 → 7); `/*` vs `*/` balanced in all 5 edited files; brand-literal scan over `App/app/src/main` reports 0 offenders; no literal `\"` artifacts in the diff (the two in `SearchScreen.kt` are pre-existing). New test `only a ready player counts as a first frame` pins the fix (IDLE/BUFFERING/ENDED false, READY true). **Done:** pushed (this commit); Android CI 42402350901 SUCCESS, APK app-debug-271 (artifact 13586316677). Device checks owner-only (fresh install → Home claim button shows no amount, claim still pays the server-confirmed coins; Search with an empty query shows the paste hint, pasting a direct link still plays; a stalled copy shows the give-up card at 30 seconds with Retry / Try another print / Open with another player). Follow-up, not built here: a mid-playback stall watchdog — once a frame has rendered, a later endless rebuffer has no deadline of its own.


## Session 100 — Server playback resolve: fail fast on dead copies (2026-11-08 ~01:00 PKT)

**What:** the player opened on Pushpa 2 The Rule (post 125647) and stayed black until the 3-minute emulator session expired — the server took 25–35 s to answer 404. Measured live, read-only: 1080p top stream id 404s in 27.4 s, 720p in 25.3 s, 480p in 35.5 s, the 1080p backup in 23.8 s; Pushpa 1 (post 141141) 720p top 404s in 8.0 s. The 1080p release (legacy releases.id 1490518) holds 8 copies, all landing pages of hosts with NO unwrapper in the resolver (clicknupload, 1fichier, 1cloudfile, gofile, multiup, hidan x2, koramaup): resolve_live_source proved the negative serially, and each failover sibling paid TWO probes of the same URL (its cached probe, then the fall-through stale probe of the identical address) — about 35 probes at ~0.7 s each. Detail says playable: true for all of them, but viewer.link_view computes playable from kind + status only — a static ledger projection, never a probe; the rows are status active (a storage default) with last_checked_at NULL, never probed, ever. Found, NOT fixed: the app's DetailViewModel.playV2 sends RankedPrint.releaseId, the LEGACY releases.id, while /api/v2/play looks it up in cat_releases — 1490518 does not exist there, so the V2 call 404s and the app falls back to the slow v1 stream route; mapping needs fuzzy title matching (cat_works.work_key is derived from title/year, the legacy post carries a different title string), a bigger design for Phase D. A transient was also noted and not chased: two public POSTs to /api/v2/play hung with no response (70 s / 30 s) while loopback answered 200 in 0.026 s in the same window; the nginx limiter returns 503, not a hang, so it is not the limiter. Fix, in Fast_Fetch/fast_fetch.py resolve_live_source only (no schema change, no new hosts, no app change, no API-shape change): (1) skip the redundant stale probe — a _cached_probe_failed_url local records the address the cached probe just failed on, and the fall-through returns the byte-identical refusal (no_playable_source / stale_probe_failed / stale: True) without re-probing it; when the failure came from elsewhere (expiry, age, dead status — no probe ran), the stale probe still runs as before; (2) bound the sibling failover with _FAILOVER_BUDGET_SECONDS = 10.0 — the Stage-2 general failover loop stops trying new siblings past 10 s from loop start; the copy the user picked keeps its full unbounded unwrap attempt (a slow bridge can take 7–42 s and still play), only the silent backups are budgeted. Worst case for a dead release is now ~12 s instead of 25–35 s — inside the app's 30 s player give-up (Session 99) and nginx's 60 s stream timeout. Nothing here makes Pushpa 2 play: its copies are unwrappable or dead, which needs new unwrappers or a fresh scrape.

**Checks:** 2 new tests in test_database.py (test_resolve_skips_second_probe_of_same_failed_address: one dead copy, exactly 1 probe, reason stale_probe_failed; test_failover_stops_after_budget: 6-copy release, faked time.monotonic, exactly 1 sibling tried, 2 probes total); test_database 140 OK, test_redact 76 OK; full backend gate 49/49 PASS (FAILED MODULES: 0). **Done:** commit f1fe4624 on tracking-a-20261107 (workspace + GitHub + VM clone, clean); Backend CI 42406365358 SUCCESS, Android CI 42406365399 SUCCESS, APK app-debug-272; gate deploy LIVE 01:02 PKT 2026-11-08 (backup 00:35 verified, page_count 1,561,748; service active since 01:02:44; posts/suggest/remote-config/login all 200). Live proof: dead Pushpa 2 1080p stream 404s in 12.45 s (was 25–35 s). Device checks owner-only (none — no app change).

## Session 101 — Resolver Phase A: negative cache, per-host timeouts, failover order, Stage-1 budget, parallel probing (2026-11-08 ~01:30 PKT)

**What:** the 01:26 PKT audit ordered five small resolver fixes before any new scraper work, and this session builds all five, backend only. **Negative cache (biggest):** download_sources gains last_failed_at + failure_class (probe_failed / no_unwrapper / unwrap_failed), in the DDL and the table_info migration beside expires_at, so old DBs gain them on first open; TTL 12h as a named constant. resolve_live_source checks it at the top, after the row load and the adult gate: a fresh record refuses with reason negative_cache and no probe at all; an expired record is cleared and the resolve proceeds. Failures are recorded at the cached-probe fail and once after the unwrap dispatch (a branch that ran and returned nothing records unwrap_failed, no branch at all records no_unwrapper); successes clear the record inline, and the stale-success path clears only after the stale probe passes. A fresh scrape writes new rows, so the cache cannot block freshly scraped copies. **Per-host timeouts:** a dead landing page cost 15s x 3 retries (45s) on the legacy http_get default; fastdl and both vcloud hops now cost one 5s fetch each, hubcloud_drive / gdflix / greenmotors keep 10s but retry once instead of twice more. **Failover order:** tiers.CANDIDATE_ORDER_SQL existed but was used by no code; it is now a function candidate_order_sql(alias) applied to all three sibling queries in resolve_live_source, so direct copies are tried before bridge copies. **Stage-1 budget:** resolve_release takes the same 10s deadline Session 100 added for Stage-2. **Parallel probing:** siblings resolve on a ThreadPoolExecutor (max 4 workers), probe-only — resolve_copy is network-only and never touches the DB, and the recursive resolver never runs in a thread. The scheduler is lazy: it submits the next sibling only while before the deadline, so the budget still means no new sibling starts past it; results are re-sorted into input order before ranking, and one sibling raising no longer aborts the whole Stage-1 failover.

**Checks:** test_database 140 -> 152 (12 new: negative-cache hit/miss + TTL expiry + unwrap_failed class, failover order, Stage-1 budget, five timeout-cap tests, parallel-probe thread proof, deadline unit test), test_tiers 35 -> 36 (candidate_order_sql alias test), test_redact 76 OK; full backend gate 49/49 PASS (FAILED MODULES: 0). Two test bugs found in testing: the suite setUp stubs classify_backend to unknown, so the resolve_release tests re-patch it to vidbolt, and classify_backend only maps vidbolt.xyz, not vidbolt.example. **Measured:** dead Pushpa 2 1080p stream 404s in 13.5s on live (read-only, 01:49 PKT, Session 100 code) — every press re-proves the negative; after this deploys, the second press within 12h should answer in milliseconds. **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges); live deploy (parent runs the gate). Deliberately left out: the V2 legacy-id / wrong-film fix (Phase D), new unwrappers (gofile/1fichier, Phase C), projection rebuild, panel changes, app code. Device checks owner-only (none — no app change).

## Session 105 — Security fixes (2026-11-08 ~12:30 PKT)

**What:** the 21-finding API/app security audit, app side (backend half lives in Fast_Fetch/checkpoints/20261108-muse-session105-security-fixes.md). **HTTPS default:** brand.properties network.default_server_url is now https://raddstream.duckdns.org; release_cleartext_for_default_host=false stays (now consistent — the release build reaches its HTTPS default). Release build also gains isDebuggable=false. **Sync key at rest:** new SyncKeyCrypto.kt — AES-GCM with a Keystore-held key (alias radd_stream_sync_key), random 12-byte IV prepended, stored as v1:+base64; API 23+ only (below 23 the key stays plaintext and the card says so); key loss fails closed (decrypt null reads as signed out, ciphertext left in place). ServerSettingsRepository encrypts on write, decrypts on read, migrates a legacy plaintext value once in the background (only if still the value seen). AndroidManifest allowBackup is now false, with a comment — Auto Backup no longer carries DataStore/Room off the device. **Copy honesty:** the reveal and link warnings are scheme-aware now (revealWarning(serverUrl), linkTransportWarning(serverUrl)) — HTTPS/TLS for the shipped default, plain-HTTP in the clear for a viewer-chosen HTTP server; RaddStreamApi KDocs and the Four rules KDoc updated the same way. **Cert pinning:** brand.properties network.tls_pins carries the 3 chain pins (intermediate YE2, Root YE, ISRG Root X2 — leaf never pinned); Brand.tlsPins accessor; ApiProvider.certificatePinnerFor (null unless host == default host and pins non-empty; malformed pin throws at build); brandNetworkPolicyXml emits a pin-set for the default host in the release config. **LAN share:** LanShareServer mints a fresh 128-bit random per start (hex, via LanShareProtocol.generateSessionToken — hex, not base64, because java.util.Base64 needs API 26 and android.util.Base64 is untestable on the JVM); the protocol checks ?token=<session> in constant time BEFORE the file-token lookup (wrong session is 403 and reveals nothing about files); shareUrl carries it; the runner stores it in LanShareStatus.Listening (never in a status line or log); a restart invalidates every URL the last session handed out. DEVIATION, owner decision owed: the brief said bind loopback only, but loopback-only kills the feature outright (the runner advertises a LAN IPv4) — shipped token-only, LAN bind kept.

**Checks:** new SyncKeyCryptoTest.kt (round-trip, distinct IVs, wrong key / tampered / garbage to null, isEncrypted, source scan that the write path encrypts and the manifest keeps backup off); ApiProviderTest +5 pinning tests; new TlsPinsTest.kt (HTTPS default, 3 pins decode to 32 bytes each, leaf absent, cleartext off); LanShareProtocolTest +5 (missing/wrong/right session, check-before-lookup, minting, URL shape). Pre-push Kotlin checks clean: no unused new imports (one removed from TlsPinsTest), no @Composable count change, comment-balance deltas are the pre-existing */$size string literal. No local Gradle run on this VM by owner order — CI (ci.yml on push) is the verification path for all four app items, so they are code-complete, NOT verified. Backend gate 50/50 PASS (1,949 tests). **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges); live deploy (parent runs the gate); release tag (owner-only). Device checks owner-only (none — no device run).

## Session 122 — Implementation: cast, search finish, disclaimer, force-update, remote-config, anti-cheat (2026-11-09 ~05:51–06:45 PKT)

**What:** the Session 122 build (base main @ 923656a; backend checkpoint in Fast_Fetch/checkpoints/20261109-muse-session122-implementation.md). **Cast + actor search (user directive, overrides "no cast in app"):** measured first — the merged catalog has NO cast column, but staging DBs do (10xflix 98%, 9xmovies 59%, desicinemas 0.2%; ~20% of posts touch a cast site, ~14% could show cast after backfill). Backend: `posts.cast_names TEXT` + migration + save (non-empty wins, never blanks), merge collects it, `post_detail` projection only, q + fuzzy search match `cast_names`, new `backfill_cast.py` (report by default, `--apply` writes, `--site` filter, joins via `post_sources` page URL). App: `PostDetail.castNames`, defensive `parseCastNames` (comma split; comma-less ≤3 words is one name, longer is glued → no cast; drops >4-word chunks, non-capitalised words except particles, digits/illegal chars; dedupe; cap 12), `DetailScreen` CastSection (chips, first 6 + "and N more", tap → actor search), omnibox label "Search for a title or an actor". No cast data → no section, never invented. **Search surface finished:** slim FilterRail (Type chips + Filters button; sheet owns the rest), tappable "Filtered" pill driven by `hasFacetFilters` (facets + years, never sort), DirectVideoRow one Play button, new PageLinkRow (page link → system browser, "No browser" note on failure), paste hint stays while the field holds a half-typed address and mentions page links, suggestions-unavailable note when the suggest call itself fails, RecentsSection (lazy row + Clear) survives under a pasted link, results in LazyColumn, group headers "N title(s)", fuzzy line names the query + "Show exact matches only" (exact-only is the empty state) + "Show close matches" back. **Disclaimer (closes DC-1):** default text VERBATIM from the owner's ordered draft (`DEFAULT_DISCLAIMER_TEXT`, version 1, radddao1@gmail.com); `PanelDisclaimer.effective()` resolves panel text when set, else the default, at read time. First run: Accept or Decline → Toast + app exits; Settings card always shows it; analytics dialog waits until the disclaimer is settled. **Force-update:** gate + dialog existed; Play Core 2.1.0 added (`app-update` + `app-update-ktx`) with new `update/InAppUpdate.kt` — flexible for soft, immediate for forced, download-page fallback on ANY Play failure (the live path today: Play only knows Play-installed builds, and this app is sideloaded); flexible download auto-completes on DOWNLOADED; immediate resumes itself if backgrounded; an immediate flow never dismisses its dialog (cancel → dialog still there). Dialog shows the published APK size when set (`apk_update_size_bytes` decoded through `AppUpdate`). **Remote-config:** server verified to emit every key the app decodes; `apk_update_sha256` deliberately NOT decoded (the app never holds the APK bytes, so a digest it cannot check is a promise it cannot keep; UPD-1b "no digest, no banner" not enforced — enforcing it would silently kill the force-update card; owner call). Remote-config content is DATA ONLY, never code. Gaps recorded, not filled: no `ads_enabled` server setting (watcher always saves ads off — matches the no-SDK state), `featureFlags.search`/`sharing` have no server knobs, top-level `wallet`/`streak` are dead keys (server publishes them only under `engagement.*` — coin screens must decode those when built). **Anti-cheat:** velocity guard in `engagement.grant_coins` (>20 credits in 600 s refuses, all reasons counted, admin exempt; rewarded-ad daily cap still binds first); a retry (duplicate idempotency key) answers idempotent BEFORE any cap/velocity check; route edge maps the refusal to 429 `rate_limited` + Retry-After 600. Device-level earnings velocity not built — `user_accounts` has no device column (`device_trials` covers trial accounts only); Play Integrity attestation is the follow-up.

**Parent fixups (06:56 PKT, after CI failed both runs on 0f29a57):** (1) Android compile error, 122's bug — `HomeScreen.kt:125` used `ActivityResult.RESULT_IN_APP_UPDATE_FAILED`, but `androidx.activity.result.ActivityResult` has no such constant; it lives in Play Core's `com.google.android.play.core.install.model.ActivityResult` (proven from the app-update-2.1.0 AAR). Fixed with an aliased import + the unused androidx import dropped. (2) Backend test flake, pre-existing (Session 105's test, untouched by 122) — the PIN `"4721"` was asserted as a bare substring, but `vip_until` is a float timestamp and at CI time it was `1794534721.0603328`, which contains `4721`; fixed to check the PIN quoted as a JSON string value. Test module re-run 89 OK. No local Gradle (owner order) — Android CI is the compile check.

**Parent fixup 3 (07:02 PKT):** the self-check read the 0f29a57 Android job log and found a SECOND compile error fixup 2 missed — `DetailScreen.kt:559 Unresolved reference 'onActorClick'`. The callback was a parameter of `DetailScreen` but line 559 sits inside the private `DetailContent`, whose parameter list had no `onActorClick` and whose call site did not pass one. Fixed by threading it through (param with default `{}` + call-site pass). NavGraph already passes the real handler (tap → actor search), so the chain is now complete. Pre-push checks clean: +3 lines only, no new imports, @Composable 15 → 16 (builder's CastSection), comment balance delta +3/+3 with the known pre-existing `"video/*"` 1-off. Pushing cancels the in-progress acebcde Android run (known to fail on this error) and starts a fresh run.

**Parent fixup 4 (07:14 PKT):** CI on 2003a93 — Backend GREEN (42548927512 SUCCESS), Android FAILED on 1 of 1147 tests: `BrandDriftTest.brandNameIsNeverHardcodedOutsideTheBrandFiles`. The build compiles now; the failure is the brand-name ratchet — 122's Decline Toast said "…to use Radd Stream.", the one new literal with the brand name outside Brand.kt/RaddMark.kt (proven by replicating the test's exact scan locally; the `"""` disclaimer block is invisible to that scanner and is not an offender). Reworded to "…to use the app." Replicated scan now shows zero offenders; pre-push checks clean (1 line, @Composable 0/0, comments 8/8).

**Landed (07:27–07:29 PKT):** CI on 310dfb0 BOTH GREEN (Backend 42550060579, Android 42550060391). APK app-debug-294 verified (24,436,379 bytes, 510 entries, manifest present). Merged to main (ff 923656a → 310dfb0, 42 files +1835/−283), GitHub main @ 310dfb0a2c, VM clone synced. Gate-deploy started with backup 0729 (PID 1313557); live-tree ff + restart + proofs to follow.

keep chip moved out of the TopBar into its own row above the scrubber; "Trying copy N of M" during the 30 s wait (new `player_attempt_counter` string, shown only when the bundle has > 1 copy). **Details:** at most one VIP upsell card (`premiumSourcesLocked` → `VipSourcesCard` only, else `!adFree` → `SponsorCard` only), "Sponsored" label dropped and title "Go VIP", and the upsell now requires a *playable* premium print — new `VipQualityGate.hasPlayablePremiumPrints()` (premium resolution AND `playableHere` OR `opensProviderPage`; ## Session 120 — UI fixes from the Session 117 device retest (2026-11-09 ~04:0x–04:30 PKT)

**What:** five UI defects from the TestingBot retest video (real Galaxy S24, APK 282, 6m50s), all app-only, base session117-player-back @ fe725d8. **(1) Give-up card overlap — root cause found:** `GiveUpCard` in `PlayerScreen.kt` put five actions in two horizontal `Row`s inside `AlertDialog`'s two button slots, which sit side by side; on a 360 dp phone they overflowed — "Open with another player" wrapped onto four lines ("Open / with / anothe / r") and "Close player" printed over its final "r". All five actions (Retry / Try another print / Open with another player / Back to details / Close player) now stack in one full-width vertical `Column` in the confirm slot, dismiss slot empty. The second error card ("Nothing playable here right now") looked clean only because it offered four buttons (no "Try another print") — the handoff card (`state.giveUp`) now offers it too when `canTryAnotherPrint`, since the deadline card already did and the viewer could otherwise only retry the same dead ladder. **(2) Search heading mismatch — a real bug, not a data quirk:** `SearchViewModel.recompute()` used `groupBy { typeGroupKey(it) }.toSortedMap(typeComparator)`; a TreeMap treats two keys as the SAME key when the comparator returns 0, and `typeOrder` only ever matched the plural labels ("Movies"), never the raw wire keys it was handed ("movie"), so every key compared equal — the map kept the FIRST key and the LAST value: a "Series" header, the Movies count, film cards underneath, exactly the retest view. New extracted `groupSearchResults(posts, sort)` groups, builds `TypeGroup`s, then `.sortedBy { typeOrder(it.label) }`; `typeOrder` now takes the label. `normalizeTypeLabel` extended (documentary → "Documentary", sport_event → "Sport event", music_video → "Music video", tv_special → "TV special", miniseries → "Miniseries" — they used to fall into "Other" while tiles painted the capitalised wire word). `WorkTypeLabel.of` in `CatalogEnums.kt` now returns "Season" for complete/ongoing_season and "Sport event"/"Music video"/"TV special"/"Miniseries" instead of raw capitalised wire ("Complete_season", "Sport_event"), so header and card agree. Live API check: `GET /api/v1/posts?q=Ali%20Baba` returns 17 rows, mixed movie/series, so the collapse is reproducible from real data. **(3) "Keeping 0 B of 3.05 GB" / playback never starts — NOT a whole-file download before play:** playback streams from the redirector at once — `PlaybackMediaItemFactory.build` sets the MediaItem URI to `rendition.playback.playUrl`, `PlaybackService.loadRendition` calls `exo.setMediaItem` + `prepare()` + `playWhenReady = true`; the keep is a background copy (auto-preview is a bounded ~40 MiB ahead-window, `AheadFetcher.DEFAULT_AHEAD_BYTES`; the whole file is fetched only after the viewer taps Keep). `OfflineCacheEngine.serveUri`'s `isComplete` gate applies only to already-stored files served via the loopback proxy, not to streaming starts. Fix: `CacheAffordance.chipText` for `Keeping` now reads "Saving a copy in the background" (+ live rate, e.g. "· 1.2 MB/s" when >0) and never quotes the full file size as a download still owed. **Why the film never started:** the Ali Baba copies were dead/slow — failover DOES run automatically: `PlaybackService.onPlayerError` feeds `FailoverStateMachine.onFailure` (retry same once per `PlaybackFailurePolicy.retrySame = 1`, then silent hop to a backup copy in the same rung, then next rank, `maxRanks = 3`), then `Handoff(NO_CANDIDATE_LEFT)` — that handoff IS the second error card seen at ~5:39 ("Nothing playable here right now / ERROR_CODE_IO_NETWORK_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT"). The timeout is an ExoPlayer network error from the source host's side (the code does not name which host; do not guess). `tryNextPrint` moves to the next rank after the active one, or a backup handle — a different print, never the same URL again — and is an honest manual hop even past max_ranks. Start deadline is 30 s (`StartDeadline.GIVE_UP_AFTER_MS`), first frame = `Player.STATE_READY` only, never a known duration. **(4) First-run ~55s black screen — two layers, both addressed as far as code allows:** (a) the window background was flat `@color/radd_ink_950` with no splashscreen library and MainActivity showed a bare spinner on black while `destination == null`. New `launch_background.xml` drawables (base: flat ink for API 21–22, where a layer-list item cannot carry gravity; `drawable-v23/`: same ink with `ic_logo_mark` centred) and `windowBackground` changed to `@drawable/launch_background` in all three `themes.xml` (base, v23, v27); MainActivity's waiting view is now a branded Column (96 dp logo mark + `Brand.name` + spinner + new string `app_loading` "Loading…"). This makes the wait visible; it does NOT shorten it — the wait itself is `trySilentDefaultConnect` (up to 15 s × 2 probes on a fresh install) plus DataStore `first()` reads, and on a slow first network that can still be long. (b) No Google sign-in is asked inside the app (observed, not a bug to fix here). **(5) Ali Baba [Tamil] greyed-out Play — CORRECT, no code fix:** live API check on post 150799: 13 releases, 13 option-group tiers, **0 playable, all mode `download_only`, sources `status: expired`, `kind: download`, `playable: false`**. `DetailScreen.kt:485` shows "No playable copy for this title yet — downloads are available." exactly when `bestPrint?.canStart != true`. The data is genuinely empty for streaming. **(6) Keyboard covering search results — cheap fix included:** `SearchScreen` had no IME handling; added `.imePadding()` to the content Column (precedent: `SetupScreen` already uses it; manifest already has `adjustResize`).

**Checks:** new `SearchGroupingTest` (4 tests: mixed rails kept, every card paints its header word, rail order Movies/Series/Seasons/Anime, null type → Other with no label); `AnimeWireTest` expectations updated from "Complete_season"/"Ongoing_season" to "Season"; new `KeepChipTextTest` (3 tests); `PlayerBackExitTest` gained 3 tests (vertical stack, all five actions present, handoff card offers try-another). Pre-push Kotlin checks clean: @Composable counts unchanged (PlayerScreen 46/46); comment balance: PlayerScreen was already 23 `/*` / 22 `*/` at HEAD (pre-existing imbalance, builder's diff adds zero `/*`); new drawables + themes + strings parse as XML (Python ElementTree OK); MainActivity imports: removed now-unused `Box`, added `Image`, `Arrangement`, `Column`, `padding`, `size`, `Text`, `painterResource`, `stringResource`, `dp` — all used; SearchScreen added only `imePadding`, used. Docs invariants 22 OK (checkpoint count 115 verified by ls). No local Gradle run on this VM by owner order — CI (ci.yml on push) is the verification path for all six app items, so they are code-complete, NOT verified. **Not done:** push (parent's step, then CI judges); device retest (parent: give-up card on a real 360 dp phone, search rails with a live mixed query, branded launch on first install, chip text during a real start, whether the Ali Baba film's copies now play at all — server-side, not an app fix). Session 121 (full-video UI audit) runs separately; its findings land after this session.
## Session 117 — Player system-Back + wrong duration (2026-11-09 ~01:25–01:45 PKT)

**What:** the two real defects from the Session 114 device retest (Sauce Labs real Pixel 5, APK 280, 01:20 PKT 2026-11-09; region switch-back PASS, VIP wording PASS). **(a) System Back never left the player:** the retest pressed phone Back once while the give-up card was up and three times during playback, and the player never exited (the top-left arrow does exit). Root cause, verified in code: the only `BackHandler` in the app lived in `EmbedWebViewScreen.kt`; `PlayerScreen.kt` had none, so during playback no handler of its own existed. This reverses a deliberate Session 114 decision — 114's entry says a root `BackHandler` in PlayerScreen was "deliberately left out (default NavController pop is correct once the card and the stacking are fixed)". The retest proves that wrong in practice on a real device: whatever the nav stack should do, a delivered back event exited nothing. Fix: one `BackHandler { onBackClick() }` in `PlayerScreenContent`, covering both entry points (stream handle and local file), registered first so a showing dialog or sheet wins the press (its handler was added later; both end back at details); the lock does not gate it. Outside-tap was verified already wired (`GiveUpCard` uses `AlertDialog(onDismissRequest = onBackToDetails)` with default dismiss-on-outside) and is pinned by test, not changed. Note for the retest: the player hides system bars with `BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE`, so in immersive fullscreen the first edge swipe is the OS revealing the bars, not a back event — standard Android behaviour, unchanged; a delivered back event now always exits. **(b) Every clock at one minute or more was wrong:** the player showed total duration "127:07:51" for the Ali Baba file. Root cause, one line in `PlayerViewModel.formatTime()` (used by position/duration, scrub toast, sleep timer, chapters, A-B points, local files): `val hours = totalSeconds / 60` divided by 60 instead of 3600 — 7,671 s / 60 = 127 "hours". Fixed to `/ 3600`; 90 s used to print "1:01:30", now "1:30". **Seasons:** no code change — the retest could not find a 2+ season title by browsing, but multi-season works exist for the retest: Family Guy work 73762 (24 seasons), One Piece 59950 (23), American Dad 72649 (22), Pokemon 74557 (20).

**Checks:** 12 new tests — new `FormatTimeTest` (8: 0 → "0:00", 45 s → "0:45", 90 s → "1:30", 59:59, 1:00:00, 1:01:01, the retest value 7,671,000 ms → "2:07:51", 24:00:00); new `PlayerBackExitTest` (4, source-drift pins: the `BackHandler` import, the call inside `PlayerScreenContent`, the give-up card's `onDismissRequest = onBackToDetails`, exactly two `BackHandler` mentions in the file). Pre-push Kotlin checks clean: import diff per file (PlayerScreen +1 `androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler`, used; PlayerViewModel unchanged), @Composable counts unchanged (PlayerScreen 46/46), `/*` vs `*/` unchanged vs base (PlayerScreen's naive 23/22 is the pre-existing string-content offset from `"*/*"` and `"video/*"`). Docs invariants 22 OK (checkpoint count 114 verified by ls). No local Gradle run on this VM by owner order — CI (ci.yml on push) is the verification path. **Not done:** push (parent's step, then CI judges); device retest (parent: system Back with card up + during playback, "Back to details" button tap, outside-tap, duration on a 2 h+ film, seasons on Family Guy/One Piece — Sauce Labs trial ~10 min left, use TestingBot 100 min reserve).

## Session 114 — App bugs found on real devices + seasons newest-first (2026-11-09 ~00:15–00:45 PKT)

**What:** three device-verified app bugs (Sauce Labs real Pixel 5, APKOnline tablet, 2026-11-08) plus a fourth item from the Session 115 findings, all app-only. **(a) Region control disappears:** after Desi → East Asian the "Watching region" row was gone from Settings — root cause was navigation, not rendering (the row has no `if` gate in code): Region `onContinue` popped back to Home, which destroyed the Settings entry underneath it (stack Home → Settings → Region). Now it pops just the Region screen, so the viewer lands back on Settings with the row visible; on a first install the region screen IS the whole stack, so Home is pushed instead (MainActivity sets startDestination = Region when the region is unanswered). The row also moved out of "Playback & Display" (10th card down) into its own "Watching region" card directly under "Catalog Server" at the top of Settings. **(b) Stuck player traps the user:** after a failed play, Back only dismissed the give-up card and the card came back — its dismiss meant "keep waiting, fresh thirty seconds" (the 200 ms ticker recomputes the 30 s deadline until a first frame) — and no Player navigation used `launchSingleTop`, so a double-tap on Play stacked two identical players and one Back landed on the same player. GiveUpCard now gains an explicit "Back to details" button and Back/outside-tap exit the player ("Close" still means keep waiting); all six player/embed navigations (Search direct, Downloads play + play-local, LocalFiles play-local, Detail play + provider page) are single-top. "Try another print" was checked and is NOT a no-op (guarded on `nextPrintHandle != null`); the "Cache engine failed" toast is a Keep-chip toast, not the retry path; no main-thread freeze was evidenced and none was chased. **(c) Inconsistent VIP presentation:** Membership said "Free plan — playback stays at 720p" while Account showed "Free VIP days are running — 3 days left". Root cause: two different facts — Membership read a one-shot entitlement (`entitlement?.vip`), Account read the live trial countdown, and `startDeviceTrial` never refreshed the entitlement after minting (refresh was only ever called from VipViewModel). The trial account IS VIP on the server (`vip_until = now + 3 days`, and the server quality gate passes it), so the Membership line was factually wrong. Fix: trial mint now forces `refreshEntitlement(force = true)`, and the Membership summary is a new pure `MembershipCardCopy.summary` (VIP dated / VIP active / payment pending wordings preserved exactly, plus a trial branch naming the days left and that 1080p/4K play until they end, then the free-plan fallback) — the Account card is unchanged and the two can never disagree again. The trial grant itself is unchanged. **(d) Seasons newest-first:** the 115 builder measured the server cannot fix this (the app re-sorts seasons ASC itself in `V1CatalogMapper.kt:101` and never calls v2 work detail), so the flip is app-side: the mapper (`.sorted()` → `.sortedDescending()`) and the display sort in `ReleaseLayoutBuilder` (`.sortedByDescending`, applied AFTER the absolute-numbering recompute, so S01E01 stays absolute 1) — episodes inside a season stay oldest-first, and the default selected season is now the newest. Deliberately left out: a root `BackHandler` in PlayerScreen (default NavController pop is correct once the card and the stacking are fixed); any change to the trial grant; server-side season work.

**Checks:** 7 new tests — ReleaseLayoutBuilderTest +1 (seasons [2, 1], absolute numbers 1/2/3, episodes within S01 [1, 2]); new V1CatalogMapperSeasonsTest (S01/S04 → [4, 1], episodes ASC); new SettingsMembershipTest (5: VIP dated, VIP active, trial names days + 1080p/4K, pending payment keeps its wording, free fallback). No new unit tests for (a) navigation or (b) the give-up dialog — no existing harness for NavGraph/Compose UI, and the brief forbids inventing one; their verification is Android CI + the parent's device retest. Pre-push Kotlin checks clean: import diff per file (only SettingsScreen: +MembershipCardCopy, −3 orphaned java.* imports from the removed `planDateLabel`), @Composable counts unchanged (NavGraph 1, SettingsScreen 11, PlayerScreen 46), `/*` vs `*/` unchanged vs base (PlayerScreen's naive 23/22 is the pre-existing string-content offset). One real slip caught in review: the VIP card first used `account.trialDays` before `val account` is declared — fixed to `state.account.trialDays`. No local Gradle run on this VM by owner order — CI (ci.yml on push) is the verification path for all four app items, so they are code-complete, NOT verified. **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges); live deploy (parent runs the gate); device retest (parent: region switch-back, failed-play Back exit, trial Membership line, newest-season landing — Sauce Labs trial ~41/60 min left, TestingBot 100 min reserve).

Effective gate: 62/62.

**Fixup 2 (2026-11-10 ~05:15 PKT, after CI on 664ec2c):** Android CI 42725282083 FAILED on 664ec2c in 1m 41s — same Gradle step, so still a compile error, not tests/lint. A full re-read of the whole branch diff (every edited file, every cross-reference: SearchSort/filter wireValues, repository signatures, FavoritePost.workRef, WatchHistoryEntity fields, reportProgress signature, PostDetail.workRef, adoptWorkRef, ResumePoint fields, nav args, PosterCard.onRemove, watch_history table name, ProgressRequest fields, `attempt` shape, dropped EndpointGaps entries — all clean) found the one remaining suspect: `V2CatalogWire.kt:107` — `id in 1..Int.MAX_VALUE` with `id: Long?`. `1..Int.MAX_VALUE` is an IntRange whose `contains` takes Int; Kotlin converts no Long to Int, so it does not compile. Fixed to explicit bounds (`id >= 1 && id <= Int.MAX_VALUE`), which keep the sentinel rule (work id > Int.MAX_VALUE maps to 0, never a truncated negative). No other `Long in IntRange` pattern anywhere in the branch. Lesson: never check a Long against an IntRange — use explicit comparisons.

**Fixup 3 (2026-11-10 ~05:30 PKT, after CI on bbfa41f):** Android CI 42726951689 FAILED on bbfa41f in 2m 04s, 0 artifacts — still compileDebugKotlin. This time the real log was pulled (VM token via `bash -ic`, never printed): exactly 10 errors, ALL `Unresolved reference 'Post'` — NavGraph.kt (4, cascade), BrowseScreen.kt:48, HomeScreen.kt:101/103/828, SearchScreen.kt:106/506. Root cause: changing `onPostClick: (Int) -> Unit` to `(Post) -> Unit` needs a `Post` import, and Browse/Home/Search never had one (they only ever used `post.*` through inferred types). MyListScreen/HeroCarousel/NavGraph already imported it and were clean. CORRECTION to fixup 2: the `Long in IntRange` line was NOT an error — the log shows no V2CatalogWire error at all, so `id in 1..Int.MAX_VALUE` compiles; the explicit-bounds form stays (identical semantics, clearer). Lesson, twice over: (1) a type-name change needs its import in every file it touches — the import diff must check USAGE, not just added import lines; (2) verify a diagnosis against the CI log before fixing — the log is reachable with the VM token, and two fixup cycles were spent on a guessed cause. Fix: +1 import (`com.raddstream.app.network.models.Post`) in BrowseScreen, HomeScreen, SearchScreen, alphabetical position.

**Fixup 4 (2026-11-10 ~05:40 PKT, after CI on d92f4d2):** Android CI 42728513043 FAILED on d92f4d2 in 4m 01s — main compiled and the APK BUILT (app-debug-314, 23,790,505 bytes; ci-reports-314 also uploaded), but `:app:compileDebugUnitTestKotlin` failed with exactly 2 errors, both in `V2CatalogWireTest.kt`: `assertFalse(post.isAnimated)` (:57) and `assertTrue(post.isAnimated)` (:70) — `assertTrue/False` take a non-null `Boolean`, `Post.isAnimated` is `Boolean?`. Fixed to `== true` comparisons. (The `assertEquals(true, …)` lines on `source.playable`/`source.resumable` are fine — the Object overload accepts `Boolean?`.)

## Session 132 — Robo run-4 a11y fixes (2026-11-10 ~14:10 PKT)

**What:** Firebase Robo run 4 (matrix-3mbjopso1odt9, APK app-debug-325) PASSED with 0 crashes but a11y only 9 (1 warning, 7 minor, 1 tip) — the 8 run-3 fixes did not all take effect in Robo's measurement, so this round verifies each fix with a test that pins the rendered outcome. Fixed 7 of 9, all description/touch-target/contrast only, no behaviour change, rating stars untouched (E-C1 stays owner-blocked). **(1) Bottom nav 32dp:** the run-3 `heightIn(min = 48.dp)` was a no-op — M3 1.3.0 (the version this app's compose-bom 2024.06.00 pins) already gives the item's Box `defaultMinSize(minHeight = 80dp)`, and 32dp is the active-indicator pill inside it, so the heightIn never reached the measured node. The item now carries `fillMaxHeight()` so its own clickable is the 80dp bar height, not the 32dp indicator; new `BottomNavTouchTargetTest` renders the bar the way NavGraph does and asserts every item's clickable node is >= 48dp (and ~80dp, the full bar). **(2–3) Player black subtitles:** the only black-text path over a black frame is the native subtitle path — `styleNativeSubtitles` called `setApplyEmbeddedStyles(true)`, letting the subtitle file's embedded styles override the viewer's bright colour (white by default, only bright colours allowed by the SubtitleColor rule) to black. Now `setApplyEmbeddedStyles(false)`, so the viewer's bright colour always wins; position/size still come from the viewer's snapshot. No direct Robolectric test is feasible (needs a real PlayerView); Robo run 5 must confirm. **(6) Sort rows:** the description is now the label only (`"Sort by Newest"`), no state words — the RadioButton's own `selected` semantics property is what TalkBack announces. The row is extracted into internal `SortOptionRow` (testable) and new `SortOptionRowTest` asserts the spoken name has no state word and the selected state is still present on the merged node. **(7) Player two "Play":** new string `player_play_centre` "Play video" for the centre control when paused (mirror of the existing `player_pause_centre` "Pause video"); the Ghost-mode edge control — the centre control by another name — says the same. **What:** CI run 42793148769 on `muse/a11y-robo4-fixes-20261110` (8464e33) FAILED at `:app:compileDebugUnitTestKotlin` — 16 errors, all in the two new tests, none in production code and none in `BottomNavTouchTargetTest` (which compiled clean). ## Session 132 fixup 3 — CI compile error: `selected = selected` reassigns the parameter (2026-11-10 ~15:15 PKT)

**What:** CI run 42796072041 on `muse/a11y-robo4-fixes-20261110` (683cba6) FAILED at `:app:compileDebugKotlin` — one error, `SearchScreen.kt:789: 'val' cannot be reassigned`. The `selected = selected` line in `SortOptionRow`'s semantics block does not assign the semantics property: the row's `selected` parameter shadows the receiver's `selected` property even on the assignment's left side, so the compiler reads it as reassigning the `val` parameter. Fix: `this.selected = selected` — the receiver is named explicitly, so the left side is the semantics property and the right side is the parameter. No other file changed. No local Gradle run by owner order — CI (ci.yml on push) is the verification path.

## Session 137 — Robo run-9 a11y fixes (2026-11-10 ~21:35 PKT)

**What:** Firebase Robo run 9 (matrix-y24kjaogxn98a, APK app-debug-343) PASSED with 0 crashes, 50 screens, 98 actions — a11y 2 → 3. The run-8 touch-target fix is verified (0, was 1). The three issues are fixed here on `muse/a11y-robo9-fixes-20261110` from main 99bfb89. **(1) Low contrast x2 (minor, Fall detail, 1.03:1, fg #030404 on bg #07090B, TextView[3]/[4] at the very top edge in the status-bar strip):** an ATF sampling artifact, the same class the run-6 session established — the sampled colours are the theme's own background (#06090B) and surface floor (#040708), not any text colour, and no Text in the app is painted dark-on-dark (verified by grep: no Color.Black anywhere in the detail screen or components). The flagged nodes are the detail header's texts caught partially scrolled under the opaque TopAppBar, where ATF samples the bar/background pixels instead of the text. The real header pairs are pinned in TextContrastTest (title 16.74:1, meta 10.05:1, included line 9.26:1, rating pill 11.08:1 — all computed from brand.properties, all above the 4.5:1 floor), so a future darkening of any of them fails here first. No layout change: hiding the header from the tree while partially scrolled would take real words away from TalkBack users to satisfy a pixel sampler, and pinning the header would only move the artifact to the next scrolling text — the artifact is inherent to any scrolling list under this checker. **(2) Content labeling x1 (tip, Media Vault, "Rank #1" identical on 2 items):** a real defect, fixed. The chain: a keep whose engine task has a blank title and a "Rank #1" print label (displayLabel's fallback when a rendition carries no quality words, set in PlaybackService) gets title "Rank #1" via the title-to-print-label fallback in toKeptItem AND keeps printLabel "Rank #1", so KeptItemRow painted "Rank #1" twice on one card; two such keeps also speak identically. Fix (the run-6 printSpokenLabel pattern): `shownPrintLabel()` paints the label only when it says something the title does not (blank or identical-to-title hides it), and `printLabelDescription()` speaks "$title — $printLabel" so two cards sharing one rank label never speak identically — painted text unchanged. Applied to both rows (KeptItemRow and DeviceDownloadRow, now internal for the test, run-8 precedent); the flagged card is a keep (FAILED: "Download failed — retry to resume" + Retry matches KeptItemRow exactly). **(3) Wrong poster (secondary observation, NOT an a11y issue):** the Fall detail screen shows the MASTERPLAN poster. DATA-SIDE, not app-side, and not changed here: Robo opened Fall post 7648 (IMDb 6.4, Thriller/Action/Drama, Becky synopsis — all match the API's /api/v1/posts/7648), and the API serves poster_url https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/original/7ULXMNvmZSi13zN3EZjz8wbER9B.jpg for it — the Masterplan poster — and that one URL is stored on 13,234 posts (the catalog's most-shared poster URL; next are 7,148 and a 5,998-post site logo). The app loads exactly what the server serves; fixing it needs a catalog poster re-resolve, a backend/data task, and catalog data is never changed from an app fix. **Not changed, per the task:** E-C1 rating stars (#9AA5FF) — owner decision, still unanswered; the 48dp bottom-nav pill (untouched); displayLabel's "Rank #1" fallback itself (the player uses it as a label; only the Downloads rows duplicated it); no backend files.

## Session 140 — Gap A2/A5/A6: playback TTFF + cache-first Home + pick-3 cold start (2026-11-11 ~07:05 PKT)

**What:** the artifact gap review's A2/A5/A6, built on `muse/gap-a2a5a6-playback-home` from main 79dd9b3. **A2 (pre-resolve):** `DetailViewModel` now resolves the best print's `playV2` bundle when the detail opens (`preresolveTopLink`, same 2 s ceiling and quality preference as the Play-press call, locked prints never warmed) and `playPrint` reuses the bundle when it is fresh (120 s) and for the same print — otherwise the old call runs exactly as before. `bestPrintOf` is the same "best" Play offers (highest `rankKey`), extracted from `playBest` so both paths cannot drift. **A2 (Wi-Fi pre-buffer):** on Wi-Fi the pre-resolved winner's first bytes are pre-buffered through the engine's bounded, silent preview seed (the same seed the player uses for the next episode); mobile data never pre-buffers, and a failed seed is silent. **A2 (honest error + Try next source) and A6 (pin Continue Watching first) and A5 (optimistic My List) were verified already built — not rebuilt:** the player already shows a `GiveUpCard` with a one-tap "Try another" (`tryNextPrint`), plus connection-error, offline and 30 s deadline cards; Home already renders Continue Watching first and pinned, above the hero; `FavoritesRepository` already writes Room first and reports to the server fire-and-forget, so the heart fills instantly. **A5 (cache-first Home):** the last good Home is kept as one JSON string in DataStore (`home_cache_json`); the ViewModel restores it before the network load, refreshes behind it, and re-saves only when the network actually returned new rows (a failed refresh never re-stamps stale rows as fresh). Panel rails are never cached. A sync/status line dates the rows ("Updated 2 min ago", offline: "Offline — showing saved Home from 2 min ago"). **A5 (My List sync line):** "Synced …" / "N titles not synced yet" / "Saved on this device", from `FavoritePost.syncedAt` and the sign-in flag — rows without a handle never count as waiting. **A6 (pick-3):** a first-run "Pick 3 you like" card in the pinned-first slot, only for a brand-new viewer (no Continue Watching, no saved titles, not done) and only when a rail offers three real titles; each heart saves to My List, the third pick or "Not now" retires it for good (persisted). **Not built, with the reason:** `DefaultPreloadManager` is playlist-driven (it preloads upcoming playlist items by distance from the playing index; verified against the Media3 API reference) and this player is single-item by design — `setMediaItem` is the only playlist call in the tree, and `STATE_ENDED` drives resume, sleep timer, auto-delete and Up Next, so a multi-item playlist would break the ended flow; the playback data source is also plain HTTP with no shared cache, so preloaded backup bytes could not be reused by a failover hop. The Wi-Fi preview seed above is the pre-buffer this architecture can actually honour. That playlist redesign (player playlist + shared `CacheDataSource`) is the follow-up if the parent wants the literal class wired.

**Checks:** full import diff per edited/added Kotlin file (all 6 added imports used; the 4 flagged — `PaddingValues`, `getValue`/`setValue` — are pre-existing and used implicitly via delegation/Scaffold; no removed imports); duplicate top-level type scan over the whole App tree: no real duplicates (the one flagged `None` is three nested `object None` declarations inside three sealed classes in the untouched `PlayerGestures.kt`; the 2 new types `CachedHomeSection`/`CachedHomePayload` each defined once); no `/*` inside KDoc added; new tests are plain JUnit (no Compose rule, so the Session 131/132 import traps do not apply); no test weakened; no backend files, no panel, no disclaimer, no E-C1 colour, no tag. New tests: `PreResolveTest` (best print + freshness rules), `HomeCacheTest` (JSON round-trip, panel rails never cached, corrupt → null), `SyncStatusTest` (relative-time buckets + both status lines), `PickThreeTest` (card visibility rules). No local Gradle run by owner order — CI (`ci.yml` on push) is the verification path. **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges); parent gate into main; no merge, no deploy.

**What:** the artifact gap review's A3/A4, built on `muse/gap-a3a4-subtitles-search` from main 36cc4d33, two commits so the A4 commit is separable at landing. **A3 (commit 5068ca6):** subtitle URLs harvested server-side into a new `subtitle_tracks` table — a resolve-time hook in `resolve_live_source` records the resolver verdict's subtitles in the same transaction, and `harvest_from_staging` maps importer `raw_meta` notes onto ledger links — and attached as `tracks[]` to every v2 bundle link via `_ordered_links` (missing → `[]`, never a missing key; a subtitle read can never 500 a bundle). The list-row "subtitled" badge now also reads stored tracks. App: wire (`WireSubtitleTrack`, `@SerializedName("subtitle_url")`) → domain (`Rendition.tracks`) → player (native Media3 tracks via the factory + SRT/VTT overlay honouring the persisted subtitle-language policy, staged per failover hop). **A4 (commit 748902b):** forgiving search — `mood_search.py` (English, Roman Urdu/Hindi and Devanagari moods → genres) and `did_you_mean.py` (one-edit correction on the real `search_fuzzy` machinery); v2 `search()` answers a pure mood query as a genre browse, searches a mixed query literally first with the mood as fallback, and serves a typo's corrected rows with `did_you_mean` + `fuzzy`; `suggest()` is typo-tolerant; v1 list search (the app omnibox path) gains the pure-mood fallback and `did_you_mean`, and reports `moods`/`mood_genres` even on a literal hit. **Already present, not rebuilt:** FTS5 index, v1 fuzzy fallback, actor matching in v1, app suggestion chips, the player persisting subtitle/audio picks. **Not built, with the reason:** cast in v2 search needs a projection change (v2 carries no cast) — follow-up; a handle-based subtitle route (`/api/v1/subtitle?id=<token>` → 302) instead of raw CDN URLs in the bundle — follow-up if the parent wants it (the bundle's `subtitle_url` is the first raw upstream URL in a bundle). **Collision found mid-build:** a sibling built A3 separately on `muse/a3-subtitle-tracks-20261111` @ 31c1ea84 (CI green, parent-verified) with a different design — `cat_subtitles` in the projection harvested from `playback_type='subtitle'` ledger rows, `tracks.subtitles` addressed by a new `GET /api/v2/subtitle/{handle}` → 302 route, no app rendering. ## Session 143 — Scrub thumbnails (API 23+ gate) + rating colour white (2026-11-11 ~20:30 PKT)

**What:** two owner-approved items from 19:33 PKT, built on `muse/scrub-thumbs-rating-20261111` from main 3baf5ab6. **(1) Scrub thumbnails, gated by API level:** the player now shows a preview picture above the seek bar while the viewer drags (gesture scrub), on API 23+ only; Android 5 (API 21-22) keeps the time-only toast, exactly as now. minSdk stays 21 — no Media3 bump. Implementation is a second, muted, always-paused ExoPlayer (`ScrubPreviewPlayer`, new `player/ScrubPreview.kt`) that renders the frame at the scrub target into the toast's TextureView; it fetches through the same data source as the main player (our user agent, cross-protocol redirects on) and seeks with CLOSEST_SYNC, like the main player. Why a second player, not MediaMetadataRetriever: most playback is HLS, and the retriever reads media FILES — it cannot open a playlist, so `getFrameAtTime` on an HLS address returns null. A server-side sprite would be the other honest route, but the backend serves none (backend task, not this one). The thumbnail only appears once the target has sat still for 300 ms (`SETTLE_MS`): during a fast drag the preview player never settles, so showing the TextureView then would show the frame from the last settled position — the wrong scene for the time the toast prints. The Scrubber (bottom bar) does not show a thumbnail — it seeks the main player directly, so the picture itself is already the preview. The preview player is created lazily on the first scrub and released when the screen leaves; it is muted, takes no audio focus, has no MediaSession and no wake lock. **(2) Rating colour (E-C1), owner decision 2026-11-11: white.** The PosterCard "★ 8.1" rating is now `Color.White` on the RaddArtScrim plate, not the purple tertiary (#9AA5FF, ~3.8:1) — white on the scrim is ~8.6:1, the easiest read. Only this rating text changes; tertiary itself (download arrow, offline badge) and the DetailScreen rating pill (onTertiaryContainer on tertiaryContainer, pinned in TextContrastTest) are untouched.

**Checks:** full import diff per edited Kotlin file (PosterCard gains `Color`, used; PlayerScreen gains `TextureView`, `ScrubPreviewPlayer`, `ScrubThumbnails`, all used; TextContrastTest gains `Color` + `RaddArtScrim`, used; ScrubPreview/ScrubPreviewTest import only what they use; no removed imports); duplicate top-level type grep over the whole App tree: `ScrubThumbnails`, `ScrubPreviewPlayer`, `ScrubPreviewTest` each defined once; no `/*` inside KDoc added; new tests are plain JUnit (no Compose rule, so the Session 131/132 import traps do not apply — no `onAllNodes`/`assertExists` imports); no test weakened. New tests: `ScrubPreviewTest` (API 21/22 no thumbnails, API 23/34 thumbnails; thumbnail shows only when settled target matches and a preview exists, hides while dragging, hides with no preview) and `TextContrastTest.posterCardRatingOnScrimMeetsContrastFloor` (white on the scrim clears 4.5:1). No local Gradle run by owner order — CI (`ci.yml` on push) is the verification path. **Not done:** push (this commit, then CI judges); parent land into main; gate deploy. The thumbnail is a live TextureView, not a captured Bitmap — on a slow connection the first frame after a settle can lag the time text, which stays correct; a debounce/frame-ready signal is the follow-up if the parent wants it tighter.

**Fixup (CI run 43041812636, ~19:58 PKT):** Android CI failed at `:app:compileDebugKotlin` — exactly one error, `ScrubPreview.kt:146:37 No value passed for parameter 'p0'`. Cause: in Media3 1.3.1 `Player.clearVideoTextureView` takes the TextureView (it identifies which view to detach), not zero arguments — my no-arg wrapper was wrong. Fix: `ScrubPreviewPlayer.clearVideoTextureView(view: TextureView)` passes the view through, and the toast's `AndroidView onRelease` now hands its view over instead of discarding it (`{ view -> previewPlayer.clearVideoTextureView(view) }`). No import changes, no new types, no test changes. Re-pushed for a fresh CI run.

**Fixup 2 (CI run 43044142835, ~20:07 PKT):** the compile error was gone, but 1 of 1362 unit tests failed — `UniversalSourceTest.theFactoryBuildsTheTypeIntoTheItemItReturns` (AssertionError at line 128). Cause: that test pins a census — the ONLY file in `src/main/kotlin` containing `MediaItem.Builder()` must be `PlaybackMediaItemFactory.kt` — and my `ScrubPreviewPlayer.setRendition` built its own MediaItem with `MediaItem.Builder()`, a second composer. Fix: the preview no longer builds a MediaItem at all. New `PlaybackMediaItemFactory.buildPreview(rendition)` composes a minimal item (URI + manifest declaration only, no mediaId/metadata/subtitles/DRM) inside the one composer file, and `setRendition` calls it. The census is back to one file; the `MediaItem` import leaves ScrubPreview.kt. No test weakened — the invariant test is the thing that caught this.

## Session 145 — Browser-identity rework + player stall watchdog + download endpoint (2026-11-12 ~07:00 PKT)

**What:** the redesign branch reworked for the streaming-app identity (browser identity dropped 06:24 PKT), on `muse/app-redesign-20261112` from 73d47681. **(1) Rework:** Home's omnibox is now a plain movie-search bar ("Search movies and shows", catalog only — no address, no web page); the `BrowserStrip` is gone, replaced by `LibraryStrip` ("Your library": "On this phone" + "Downloads" only, no paste-a-link door); Search no longer offers any open-in-browser row — a pasted web page gets the plain line "Web links don't play in Radd. Search the catalog for the title instead." and the paste hint drops the browser half; pasting a direct .mp4/.m3u8 link still plays in-app (DirectVideoRow, unchanged). All 55 design fixes are kept (layout, titles, ratings, posters, quality card, Settings, VIP, search, Live TV cache). Tests updated: `HomeSectionMarginsTest` renders `LibraryStrip`, `TextContrastTest` library-tile name. **(2) Player — stall watchdog:** diagnosed the play path end to end (detail `?warm=1` + `playV2` 2 s ceiling + v1 fallback + `PlaybackService` `setMediaItem`/`prepare`/`playWhenReady` + `DefaultHttpDataSource` cross-protocol redirects). The one verifiable defect: a load that never errors and never renders had NO failover trigger — the error path only fires on a player error, so a hung resolve/stalled CDN sat on the "Warming the link" notice until the 30 s card and nothing ever advanced it. Fix: `StartDeadline.START_WATCHDOG_MS = 8_000` + a watchdog job in `PlaybackService` armed per load, disarmed on first frame (READY), firing `FailoverStateMachine.onFailure` exactly like a player error (retry same once, silent hop, next rank, honest give-up) — worst case to the give-up card is now ~17 s, not 30 s of nothing. Also `PlayerViewModel.startStreamHandle` caps the resume seed at 5 s (`withTimeoutOrNull`) so a hung sync lookup can never hold back Play. Live API verified while diagnosing: Salaar 79874 detail 200 (3 releases, 3 tiers), 720p `/api/v1/stream` 302s to a real video file, `/api/v1/download` 302s the same way. **(3) Download:** in-app device downloads now fetch through `/api/v1/download?id=<handle>` (new `downloadRedirectUrl` helper, VIP pass rides it) instead of the play redirector; `DownloadService` (OkHttp, Range resume, foreground notification, MediaStore) is unchanged in behaviour. The HLS keep-engine leg still fetches its playlist through the play redirector (segmented keep, not a file download). The ShrinkMe web link stays only as the separate secondary row in the download dialog, never the Download button's action.

**Checks:** full import diff per edited Kotlin file (to run after commit — script compares main...HEAD); duplicate top-level type grep over the whole App tree (to run after commit); no `/*` inside KDoc added; new tests: `StartDeadlineTest` watchdog pin (8 s < 30 s, stage ACTIONS at 8 s), `ApiProviderTest` download-URL pin (trailing-slash trim + handle encoding).
